23:T4cf,<p><b>Dragon Ball Z </b>Action Figure of <b>Frieza Third Form </b>in <b>S.H. Figuarts </b>collection, size approx.15cm. <b>Official product</b> by <strong>Tamashii Nations</strong>.</p>
<p>From "<strong>Dragon Ball Z</strong>", <strong>Frieza Third Form</strong> joins <strong>S.H.Figuarts</strong>! Featuring numerous optional hands, effects, and expression parts that can be combined to re-create your favorite scenes, such as an optional head with open eyes that's a great match for the optional ""smiling"" expression. And the set includes a glowing version Namekian Dragon Ball (six stars)! Collect all the glowing versions (the others are included with the S.H.Figuarts Frieza Fourth Form, Dodoria, Zarbon, Son Gohan (Battle Armor), and Krillin (Battle Armor), all sold separately.) </p>
<p><span style="text-decoration:underline;"><strong> Content :</strong></span></p>
<ul>
<li>Main Body</li>
<li>Three pairs of optional hands</li>
<li>Two optional expression parts</li>
<li>Optional head</li>
<li>Effect parts</li>
<li>Namekian Dragon Ball (six star) glowing version</li>
<li>Optional crossed arms</li>
</ul>
<p>This product is not a toy or product for children. It is a collectors item intended for adults only.</p>24:T6ae,<p><b>Dragon Ball Z </b>Action Figure of <b>Nail </b>in <strong>S.H. Figuarts </strong>collection, size approx.16cm. <b>Official product</b> by <strong>Tamashii Nations</strong>.</p>
<p>The <strong>Dragon Ball Z</strong> figures from <strong>S.H. Figuarts</strong> have been designed with a mobile structure that allows a wide range of images from the series to be recreated. The cape is made up of flexible parts and is also removable.</p>
<p>Interchangeable head parts, with expressions such as clenched teeth and screams, also allow the typical appearance of <strong>Nail</strong> in battle scenes to be reproduced. A defeated face is also included, allowing it to be combined with the S.H Figuarts <strong>Frieza First Form &amp; Frieza Pod</strong> (<a href="https://www.lacitedesnuages.be/en/13107-dragon-ball-z-sh-figuarts-action-figure-frieza-first-form-frieza-pod-set-11cm.html">sold separately</a>) to recreate the scene where Frieza rips off Nail's arm. In addition, part of the arm made of transparent material can be used for the following scene in which Nail regenerates the arm that Frieza ripped off.</p>
<p>This figure also comes with extra head parts to recreate the fusion of <strong>Nail and Piccolo</strong> when you also own the S.H. Figuarts Piccolo Proud Namekian figure (<a href="https://www.lacitedesnuages.be/en/13143-dragon-ball-z-sh-figuarts-action-figure-piccolo-the-proud-namekian-16-cm.html">sold separately</a>).</p>

<p>Genya Shinazugawa, the lone warrior at the heart of the Demon Slayer series, is much more than just a demon slayer. The younger brother of Sanemi Shinazugawa, the Pillar of the Wind, Genya is a complex character, marked by a tragic childhood that led him to become a demon hunter. Gifted with an iron will and unfailing determination, he stands out for his unique ability to consume demon parts to borrow their powers, a technique that makes him as powerful as he is enigmatic. His quest for vengeance leads him down a path strewn with pitfalls, but it's his evolution, his courage in the face of adversity, and his decisive contribution to the fight against Muzan Kibutsuji that capture the hearts of fans. Genya, a tragic hero, demonstrates that beyond strength, it is the power of the human spirit that is most remarkable.</p>
